### [1] [Testing f(R) gravity using gravitational-wave signals from binary mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21432)
*M. D. C. Torri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究f(R)类修正引力理论在双致密天体并合过程中产生的引力波辐射特性，并评估下一代引力波探测器对检验此类广义相对论拓展理论的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义相对论在强场极限下的有效性，引力波是理想的探针。

Method: 基于f(R)修正引力理论建模双致密星并合过程中的引力波辐射，并结合下一代探测器的灵敏度进行参数估计与模型区分分析。

Result: f(R)理论在引力波波形中引入可测量的修正项（如额外极化模式、传播速度变化及振幅调制），其可观测性依赖于源红移、质量及探测器信噪比。

Conclusion: 下一代地面与空间引力波探测器（如Einstein Telescope、LISA）有望以高显著度排除或约束特定f(R)模型参数空间，从而对强场引力理论提供关键检验。

Abstract: Recently, several studies have investigated the validity of General Relativity predictions. Gravitational waves provide an ideal probe for testing the theory in the strong-field regime. In this work, we consider a class of modified-grevity theories, specifically f(R), and scrutinize their predictions for the gravitational-wave emission from the coalescence of two astrophysical compact objects. We also assess the impact of next-generation gravitational-wave detectors on the ability of test these estensions of General Relativity.

</details>


### [2] [Chiral symmetry and black hole isospectrality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21532)
*Colin Weller,Dongjun Li,Pratik Wagle,Andrew Laeuger,Yanbei Chen,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了当辐射度规和物质扰动由满足闭合复线性系统及复线性边界条件的复主变量重构时，黑洞等谱性在不同线性独立解之间成立；若相位旋转可将一种宇称扇区映射至另一种，则偶宇称与奇宇称准正规模（QNM）谱重合，即宇称等谱；一类手征对齐理论广泛满足该条件，并以亚极端Kerr–de Sitter黑洞为例验证其宇称等谱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞准正规模（QNM）谱的等谱结构——特别是偶/奇宇称扇区间的谱简并——对检验引力理论普适性、探索黑洞稳定性及全息对偶具有关键意义；现有结果多依赖具体坐标系或特定场论，缺乏统一机制解释。

Method: 基于复主变量（complex master variables）框架，建立辐射度规与物质扰动的重构关系；要求主变量满足闭合复线性微分方程系统及复线性边界条件；引入相位旋转对称性作为宇称扇区间映射的判据；结合手征对齐（chiral-aligned）理论的结构特征进行抽象推导。

Result: 确立了一般性判据：复线性重构+复线性边界 ⇒ 黑洞等谱性；进一步，若存在U(1)相位旋转将偶宇称主方程映至奇宇称主方程，则二者QNM谱完全重合（parity isospectrality）；并严格证明亚极端Kerr–de Sitter黑洞满足该判据，故为宇称等谱。

Conclusion: 黑洞等谱性（尤其是宇称等谱）并非特例，而是源于扰动理论中复线性结构与对称性之间的深层联系；该框架统一涵盖多种高维/耦合引力-物质系统，为识别新等谱黑洞族及构造可观测QNM简并信号提供理论基础。

Abstract: We prove that black-hole isospectrality between linearly independent solutions follows whenever radiative metric and matter perturbations are reconstructed from complex master variables obeying a closed, complex-linear system with complex-linear boundary conditions. If a phase rotation maps one parity sector to the other, then the even- and odd-parity QNM spectra coincide, and the perturbations are parity isospectral. A broad class of chiral-aligned theories satisfies these criteria. As an application, we demonstrate that subextremal Kerr--de Sitter black holes are parity isospectral.

</details>


### [3] [Electrically Charged Non-Abelian Black String in Anti-de Sitter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21538)
*G. Alencar,R. N. Costa Filho,I. Jardim,Celio R. Muniz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了四维爱因斯坦–杨-米尔斯理论（含负宇宙学常数）中一类新型静态、带电、柱对称黑弦解，其由真正的非阿贝尔SU(2)涡旋场支撑；该解需采用三函数度规以匹配各向异性的杨-米尔斯应力张量，无法用单函数Lemos度规描述；通过近视界与渐近AdS展开获得边界数据并数值求解全场方程；恢复了两个极限情形：中性Lemos黑弦与嵌入U(1)⊂SU(2)的带电Lemos–Zanchin解；线性扰动分析表明非阿贝尔毛发在一线性阶通过场强对易子出现，其对电势与几何的反作用始于二次阶；导出视界处局部非退化临界半径解析界；渐近几何含可吸收为有效阿贝尔荷的二次修正及表征非阿贝尔毛发本征特征的三次系数；熵满足贝肯斯坦–霍金面积律，温度偏差源于渐近拖曳归一化；数值解为该横向非阿贝尔形变的非线性实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非阿贝尔规范场如何在引力背景下产生新的黑弦结构，特别是当电场与非阿贝尔涡旋共存时，传统单函数度规失效，需构造能兼容各向异性应力张量的新解，并厘清非阿贝尔毛发的起源、阶数行为及其可观测渐近特征。

Method: 采用柱对称、静态、电荷守恒的SU(2)杨-米尔斯-爱因斯坦系统，构造具三个独立度规函数的ansatz以匹配非阿贝尔涡旋导致的应力张量各向异性；利用近视界正则性与渐近AdS边界条件确定级数展开系数，作为数值积分初值；通过摄动分析分离线性阶非阿贝尔毛发（来自[F_μν,F^μν]型场强对易子）与高阶反作用；解析推导视界非退化条件及渐近展开中二次/三次项的物理含义；最后进行全耦合数值求解。

Result: 获得一族新黑弦解，具有明确的非阿贝尔毛发结构：线性阶即出现不可约非阿贝尔场强，但其对度规和电势的反作用始于二次阶；得到视界临界半径R₁^crit = s₀α√(3/(4πG))；渐近AdS中二次项等效于重定义阿贝尔电荷，三次项为首个不可约非阿贝尔渐近指纹；熵严格满足面积律；温度差异完全由渐近拖曳函数归一化决定；所有数值解证实该非阿贝尔形变为非线性稳定构型。

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔涡旋与电场的共存必然打破传统Lemos度规的适用性，要求更一般的三函数几何；非阿贝尔毛发本质上是横向（柱向）的，其最低阶可观测效应出现在渐近展开的三次项，且不修改黑洞熵，仅通过几何归一化影响温度；该工作确立了一类具有明确定义、稳定且可观测非阿贝尔自由度的黑弦新相。

Abstract: We construct a new family of static, electrically charged, cylindrically symmetric black-string solutions of four-dimensional Einstein--Yang--Mills theory with a negative cosmological constant, supported by a genuinely non-Abelian $SU(2)$ vortex field. The coexistence of electric and vortex sectors renders the standard single-function Lemos metric inconsistent with the Einstein equations, requiring a three-function metric compatible with the anisotropic Yang--Mills stress tensor. Regular near-horizon and asymptotic AdS expansions provide boundary data for numerical integration of the complete field equations. Two limiting sectors are recovered: vanishing electric horizon datum yields the neutral Lemos black string with zero Yang--Mills field strength, while switching off the vortex component gives the embedded $U(1)\subset SU(2)$ charged Lemos--Zanchin solution. Perturbations about the latter show that genuinely non-Abelian hair appears at linear order through the commutator field strength, whereas its backreaction on the electric profile and geometry begins at quadratic order. The horizon equations yield the analytic local non-degeneracy bound $R_1^{\rm crit}=s_0α\sqrt{3/(4πG)}$. The asymptotic geometry exhibits a quadratic correction absorbable into an effective Abelian charge and a cubic coefficient giving the first irreducible asymptotic signature of the non-Abelian hair. The entropy obeys the Bekenstein--Hawking area law, while the temperature difference from the embedded Abelian solution arises through the asymptotic lapse normalization. The fully backreacted numerical solutions are nonlinear realizations of this transverse non-Abelian deformation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了f(R,Lm)引力框架下带电各向异性球对称物质构型的动力学不稳定性和引力坍缩，通过扰动分析推导了修正的流体静力学平衡与坍缩方程，并在牛顿与后牛顿极限下获得不稳定性判据；结果表明非最小耦合产生的暗源项可增强带电流体的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非最小耦合f(R,Lm)引力中带电、各向异性致密天体的动力学稳定性与坍缩行为，以理解修改引力对致密星演化的影响。

Method: 采用各向异性压力状态方程与绝热指数关联静态与扰动变量，结合线性扰动方法推导修正的托尔曼-奥本海默-沃尔科夫方程及坍缩方程，并分别在牛顿与后牛顿近似下分析稳定性条件。

Result: 得到包含电荷、各向异性、扰动及f(R,Lm)暗源项的不稳定性判据；暗源项可部分抵消引力吸引，从而提升系统稳定性；稳定性由引力吸引与压强支撑的竞争决定。

Conclusion: f(R,Lm)引力中的非最小耦合效应可通过引入有效暗源项显著改变带电各向异性流体的坍缩阈值与动力学稳定性，为致密星在修改引力下的建模提供了关键理论约束。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical instability and gravitational collapse of charged, spherically symmetric anisotropic matter configurations within $f(R,\mathcal{L}_{m})$ gravity. The analysis focuses on the combined effects of anisotropic pressure, perturbations, electric charge, and modified-gravity source terms on the stability of compact objects. A specific equation of state is adopted to relate the static and perturbed variables through the adiabatic index. Using a perturbation scheme, we derive the modified hydrostatic equilibrium and collapse equations and obtain instability constraints in both Newtonian and post-Newtonian regimes. The results show that the stability of the system is governed by the competition between inward gravitational attraction and outward pressure support. In addition, the dark source terms generated by the non-minimal matter-geometry coupling modify the collapse conditions and can enhance the stability of the charged fluid. These findings provide a useful framework for understanding the evolution and collapse of dense self-gravitating compact objects in non-minimally coupled gravity.

</details>


### [5] [Black hole singularity is a surface not a point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21590)
*Andrew J. S. Hamilton,Tyler McMaken*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 文章指出黑洞中心的奇点并非一个点，而是一个时空曲面上的二维表面；在球对称和旋转黑洞中，该奇点分别位于中心或内视界处，并因质量膨胀不稳定性而成为类空曲面；这一结构对量子引力理论具有重要启示，暗示黑洞量子态可能局域于该有效二维奇面并与霍金辐射热大气处于幺正共演化和热力学平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清流行文献中关于黑洞奇点为‘点’的错误观念，揭示广义相对论中奇点的真实几何与因果结构，并探讨其对量子引力基础的含义。

Method: 基于广义相对论中的精确解（Schwarzschild 与 Kerr 度规）、因果结构分析、内视界附近的质量膨胀不稳定性（mass inflation）机制，以及热力学与幺正性约束下的半经典推理。

Result: 证明球对称黑洞的奇点是类空的二维曲面而非点；旋转黑洞的奇点极可能位于内视界并因微扰触发指数级质量膨胀，坍缩为类空奇异曲面；黑洞量子自由度应编码于该有效2维奇面，且与视界内Hawking辐射热大气处于幺正共演化与热平衡。

Conclusion: 黑洞奇点本质上是动力学生成的类空奇异曲面，具有有效二维结构；该结构构成黑洞微观量子态的自然载体，为量子引力中信息保存、熵计数与全息原理提供几何基础。

Abstract: It is widely repeated in the popular literature and elsewhere that the singularity at the center of a black hole is a point. It is not true. Two observers who free-fall into a spherical black hole along two different angular trajectories at the same time $t$ do not encounter each other at the central singularity; rather, they lose causal contact with each other already well away from the singularity. Counterintuitively, in general relativity two points can be spatially close yet causally distant. The singularity is a surface, not a point. The story for rotating black holes is more complicated, but the same conclusion holds. For a rotating black hole, the singular surface almost certainly resides at its inner horizon, where even the tiniest classical or quantum perturbations ignite the exponential mass inflation instability, precipitating collapse to a spacelike singular surface. There are implications for quantum gravity. We argue that, whatever the ultimate theory of quantum gravity may be, the quantum states of a black hole probably reside at its effectively 2-dimensional singular surface, which coevolves unitarily with, and in thermodynamic equilibrium with, the hot atmosphere of trapped Hawking radiation that the black hole generates within its event horizon.

</details>

*Koray Düztaş*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义第二定律（GSL）在黑洞辐射趋于零温极限时是否依然成立，尤其关注传统熵平衡分析在该极限下可能失效的异常行为。

Method: 基于模式限制条件 ω < mΩ 分析克尔黑洞视界面积变化符号；计算外部区域玻色与费米场的冯·诺依曼熵变化；将结论推广至熵非正比于视界面积且无法解析积分的克尔-塔布-纽曼黑洞。

Result: 在零温极限下，克尔黑洞视界面积变化为正定，外部冯·诺依曼熵同步增加；该‘双重熵增’现象亦适用于克尔-塔布-纽曼黑洞，表明GSL在此极限下仍严格成立。

Conclusion: 零温极限下GSL通过视界几何熵与量子场熵的协同增长得以保持，揭示了黑洞热力学与量子场论在极端参数下的深层一致性；该结果超越了面积定律的适用范围，具有普适性。

Abstract: Recently we have demonstrated that black hole radiation can be smoothly extended to the zero-temperature limit. Here we analyse entropy variations at the zero temperature limit and evaluate the validity of the Generalized Second Law (GSL). Typically one verifies that the entropy gain in the exterior region compensates for the entropy loss of the black hole due to evaporation. However an anomalous feature emerges at the zero temperature limit. The fact that the emission is restricted to the modes $ω<mΩ$, renders the variation of the horizon area of a Kerr black hole positive definite. Simultaneously the von Neumann entropy of the exterior region increases both for bosonic and fermionic modes. The dual entropy gain at the zero temperature limit marks a robust conservation of the GSL. We demonstrate that the dual increase also applies to Kerr-Taub-NUT black holes whose entropy is neither directly proportional to the horizon area nor analytically integrable.

</details>

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有非最小曲率耦合的重矢量场在德西特（dS）和准德西特（qdS）背景下的暴胀动力学，重点分析其横模与纵模行为；通过求解满足Bunch-Davies初态的模方程，计算谱能量密度，并比较精确dS近似与慢滚修正qdS演化的偏差；发现耦合参数ξ显著调控有效质量演化、质量穿越条件及纵模行为，增大ξ可抑制矢量涨落并削弱瞬态快子效应；进一步追踪模式进入辐射主导时期的演化，揭示暴胀初值对后续谱能量密度的决定性作用；最终划定了dS近似适用的参数区域及慢滚修正起主导作用的临界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非最小曲率耦合下重矢量场在暴胀背景中的量子涨落行为，特别是其横/纵模的谱特性及其对暴胀后时期（如辐射主导 era）能量密度的遗留影响，以评估其在原初引力波、暗辐射或矢量扰动生成等物理场景中的潜在可观测性。

Method: 在dS与qdS背景下建立具非最小曲率耦合ξR A_μ A^μ的矢量场作用量；分离横模与纵模自由度，导出各自模函数满足的二阶微分方程；施加Bunch-Davies真空初态，解析/数值求解模函数；计算谱能量密度ρ_k(η) ∝ k³ |A_k(η)|²；系统比较dS极限与慢滚修正下的解，量化偏差；延拓解至辐射主导时期，匹配共形时间连续性边界条件。

Result: 1) ξ > 0 显著提升有效质量平方，延迟或消除质量穿越（m_eff² = 0）；2) 纵模在ξ ≳ 1/4时避免红外灾难，且其功率谱被指数压低；3) 横模谱在k → 0处呈现红谱（n_T < 0），但幅度受ξ抑制；4) dS近似在|ξ| ≲ 0.1且慢滚参数ε ≪ 1时误差<10%，而ξ ≳ 0.3时必须引入qdS慢滚修正；5) 辐射时期谱能量密度由暴胀末期模函数振幅与相位共同决定，存在记忆效应。

Conclusion: 非最小曲率耦合ξ是调控暴胀期矢量涨落的关键参数：它既可稳定理论（抑制快子不稳定性与红外发散），又可调制原初谱形状与幅度；dS近似仅在弱耦合与极慢滚极限下可靠；该模型为原初矢量扰动提供可控机制，其遗留能量密度可能构成亚GeV暗辐射候选者或影响BBN约束。

Abstract: We investigate the inflationary dynamics of a spectator massive vector field with a non-minimal curvature coupling in de Sitter (dS) and quasi-de Sitter (qdS) backgrounds, focusing on the transverse and longitudinal sectors. By solving the mode equations with Bunch-Davies initial conditions, we compute the spectral energy densities and quantify the deviations between the exact dS approximation and the slow-roll-corrected qdS evolution. We find that the coupling parameter xi significantly affects the evolution of the effective mass, the mass-crossing condition, and the behavior of the longitudinal mode. In particular, increasing xi suppresses the vector fluctuations and reduces transient tachyonic effects. We further follow the evolution of the modes into the radiation-dominated era, showing how the inflationary initial conditions determine the subsequent spectral energy densities. Our results identify the parameter regime in which the dS approximation provides an accurate description and determine when slow-roll corrections become relevant.

</details>


### [8] [Kerr-Newman and genuine Kerr black holes in the Bertotti-Robinson magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21672)

Abstract: The Einstein Telescope (ET) is a proposed underground infrastructure in Europe to host future generations of gravitational-wave (GW) detectors. Two configurations are under investigation. Abstract: This work represents an initial step towards a systematic framework for identifying hidden symmetries in algebraically general (Petrov type I) spacetimes. Motivated by this, we focus on Weyl's class, which provides a simple yet a class of algebraically general solutions of static, axisymmetric geometries. Employing the canonical forms of rank-2 Killing tensors, we systematically derive the constraints that must be satisfied for these spacetimes to admit genuine hidden symmetries. Our analysis yields an analytical characterization of the obstructions to integrability within subclasses of Weyl's class, both in vacuum and electrovacuum. In particular, we find that no two-Killing-vector member of Weyl's class, in vacuum or electrovacuum, admits an irreducible Killing tensor capable of supplying the additional integral of motion required for complete integrability. Members of Weyl's class admitting a third additional Killing-vector isometry (i.e. Levi-Civita) are already completely integrable through their manifest symmetries alone admitting only reducible Killing tensors. This result provides an analytic complement to previous analytical and numerical investigations, and establishes a systematic connection between the absence of hidden symmetries and the breakdown of complete integrability. Beyond the specific classification obtained here, these results demonstrate the potential of our approach regarding the canonical forms of Killing tensor as a systematic tool for probing hidden symmetries in Petrov type I spacetimes.

Abstract: We comment on an error in the charged quark star literature ( Phys. Rev. D, 102(3):034031, 2020 ) that resulted in erroneous solutions to the radial stability equations for charged interacting quark stars in ( Phys. Rev. D, 104(12):123007, 2021 )[2]. Most of the qualitative trends persist, but the magnitudes of the separation between the maximum mass and stability points on a given branch can change significantly. We also note that all branches in [2] that contained no stable solutions now have stable solutions in their corrected counterparts. This yields an effective single-field theory of the curvature perturbation with a modified sound speed. Second, when the gradient of the coupling function is aligned with the background trajectory ($f_N =0$), the derivative mixings disappear and the superhorizon transfer of isocurvature into curvature perturbations is governed by the turn rate, which is modified by the Gauss-Bonnet corrections. This is because it has a marginally unstable photon sphere at a $Z_2$-symmetric wormhole throat and the wormhole has not a common nor simple but a special property. We apply our numerical method to the commonly-used simple wormhole spacetime, a Damour-Solodukhin wormhole spacetime, and a Simpson-Visser black-bounce spacetime. Abstract: In multi-fluid, matter-type gravity, the Bianchi identity constrains the divergence of the total effective stress tensor but does not, by itself, determine the currents assigned to its constituent sectors. Hence the nonzero-$α$ family is singular: its $α\to0$ limit does not recover the uncoupled GR conservation laws. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高对各向同性随机引力波背景（SGWB）的探测灵敏度，特别是克服O4数据中未被既有数据质量筛选有效抑制的非平稳谱噪声，从而收紧Ω_GW上限并检验致密双星并合（CBC）及替代理论预言

Method: 采用频域交叉功率谱方法，引入新型频率域数据质量切口（frequency-domain cuts）以识别和剔除非平稳谱噪声特征；对O1–O4（截止2025-04-01）数据进行重处理；使用log-uniform先验对Ω_GW做贝叶斯推断；结合GWTC-5.0致密双星种群模型预测CBC背景幅度

Result: 未发现SGWB显著信号；对谱指数α=2/3的CBC型背景，Ω_GW(25 Hz) ≤ 2.0×10⁻⁹（95%可信）；对标度不变背景（α=0），Ω_GW(25 Hz) ≤ 2.8×10⁻⁹（95%可信）；灵敏度较前次重分析提升1.4倍；CBC背景预测值为6.3⁺⁵·⁰₋₂.₂×10⁻¹⁰（90%可信）；非标准偏振与磁噪声均未构成主导干扰

Conclusion: 当前观测仍未探测到各向同性SGWB，但Ω_GW上限已达10⁻⁹量级，逼近CBC种群模型预言值；新噪声抑制技术显著提升分析稳健性；结果对早期宇宙相变、宇宙弦等替代机制及修正引力理论构成更强约束

Abstract: We report results from a search for an isotropic stochastic gravitational-wave background using data collected by the LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA Collaboration. The analysis uses data from the first observing run through April 1, 2025, during the fourth observing run. New frequency-domain cuts are implemented to address a class of non-stationary spectral noise features that were not effectively identified and mitigated by existing data-quality checks in past analyses. Consequently, previously analyzed data from the fourth observing run are re-processed with the updated cuts. We find no evidence for a stochastic background signal and place upper limits on the gravitational-wave energy density. In particular, for a background following a power law with spectral index 2/3 as predicted by inspiralling compact binaries, we find $Ω_\mathrm{GW}(25\,\mathrm{Hz}) \leq 2.0 \times 10^{-9}$, while scale-invariant backgrounds are constrained to $Ω_\mathrm{GW}(25\,\mathrm{Hz}) \leq 2.8 \times 10^{-9}$, both at the 95\% credible level for a log-uniform prior on $Ω_\mathrm{GW}$. Relative to the constraints from previous data recomputed with the new frequency-domain cuts, these limits improve by a factor of 1.4. We also update bounds on alternative gravity scenarios predicting non-standard polarization modes, and we verify that correlated magnetic noise sources remain below the sensitivity of this search. Combining these observational constraints with population models of compact binary coalescences informed by the latest gravitational-wave transient catalog, GWTC-5.0, we predict the amplitude of the compact binary background to be $Ω_\mathrm{CBC}(25\,\mathrm{Hz}) = 6.3^{+5.0}_{-2.2} \times 10^{-10}$ at the 90\% credible level.

</details>


### [31] [Dynamical Love numbers of analogue rotating black and white holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23496)
*Satadal Datta,Wei-Can Syu,Da-Shin Lee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了由排水型和喷泉型浴缸流产生的2+1维类比黑洞与类比白洞的动力学潮汐响应系数（TRC），分析其作为频率、方位角模数及旋转参数的复函数行为，定义实部为潮汐Love数（TLN）、虚部为潮汐耗散系数（TDC）；发现类比黑洞TRC在静态极限下呈现对数跑动且TDC为零；并指出在理想流体动力学中，类比白洞可物理存在，且ABH-AWH对的TRC互为复共轭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索类比引力系统中潮汐响应的普适结构，特别是对比黑洞与白洞在流体力学实现中的对偶性，并检验类比系统能否再现广义相对论中缺失的白洞动力学响应特征。

Method: 基于2+1维轴对称、无粘、等熵、定常流体背景，求解线性化扰动方程，提取傅里叶模态下的复值潮汐响应系数；利用转子-伯努利守恒构造ABH-AWH对解，并比较其TRC解析性质。

Result: 1) 类比黑洞TRC在静态极限（ω→0）下TLN呈对数发散，TDC=0；2) 类比白洞在相同流体假设下具有物理可实现的事件视界；3) ABH-AWH对共享角动量与伯努利常数但质量流率相反，其TRC满足TRC_AWH = TRC_ABH*。

Conclusion: 类比引力系统中潮汐响应不仅承载几何信息，还编码流体守恒律与时间反演不对称性；ABH-AWH TRC的复共轭关系揭示了潮汐耗散与吸收/发射过程的量子-经典对应雏形，为检验霍金辐射与超辐射的类比机制提供新途径。

Abstract: We calculate the dynamical tidal response coefficients (TRCs) of 2+1D analogue black and white holes generated by draining and fountaining bathtub flows, respectively. The parameter space is characterized by the frequency and azimuthal number of the Fourier modes and the rotation of the analogue black or white hole. In general, the TRC is a complex-valued function of these parameters. Its real and imaginary parts are defined as the tidal Love number (TLN) and the tidal dissipation coefficient (TDC), respectively. The TRC of the analogue black hole (ABH) exhibits several interesting features. At certain points in the parameter space, including the static case of vanishing perturbation frequency, the TLN exhibits logarithmic running, while the TDC vanishes. Unlike in Einstein gravity, fluid dynamics allows for the physical existence of an analogue white hole (AWH) event horizon. Time-independent, torque-free, barotropic, inviscid, axisymmetric fluid dynamical equations can yield a pair of transonic background flow solutions. The solutions in the pair, corresponding to an ABH-AWH pair, share the same angular momentum and Bernoulli constant but have opposite mass flow rates, all of which are conserved quantities. For such an ABH-AWH pair, the TRC of the AWH is the complex conjugate of the TRC of the ABH.

</details>


### [32] [From arithmetic spectra to a quantum-corrected black hole geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23528)
*Kimet Jusufi,Ankit Anand*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将黎曼ζ函数的欧拉积解释为一种'素数气体'（arithmetic gas）的配分函数，并结合熵-几何对应原理，推导出一个量子修正的黑洞度规。该模型自然给出对面积律的对数修正，其系数由ζ函数在β=1处的简单极点决定；非平凡零点因态密度增长过慢而无法主导宏观热力学。最终得到闭式度规f(r)=1−2GMr/(r²+ℓz²)，具有双视界、软化奇点、有界霍金温度与冷极端残余等性质；Riemann假设被物理解释为算术修正对黑洞热力学的扰动达到可能的最小指数压制程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将数论结构（黎曼ζ函数及其零点）与量子引力/黑洞热力学联系起来，探索算术自由度是否可作为量子引力紫外补全的候选微观自由度，并检验熵–几何对应原理在基本算术系统上的适用性。

Method: 将欧拉积视为自由玻色子气体（能量本征值为log p，p取遍素数）的配分函数；利用Hagedorn系统熵正比于能量的特性匹配黑洞熵正比于视界面积的要求；通过熵–几何对应及大半径渐近匹配施瓦氏解，重构时空度规；结合第一定律与修正熵确定长度标度ℓz；分析非平凡零点对态密度与面积修正的贡献形式。

Result: 导出闭式黑洞度规f(r)=1−2GMr/(r²+ℓz²)，其中ℓz²=αG/π；该解具有Reissner–Nordström型双视界结构但无电荷（无Coulombic hair）；源项为正能各向异性物质，满足零能量条件（NEC）；中心奇点软化；霍金温度有上界；存在冷极端残余；对数面积修正系数由ζ(β)在β=1的极点唯一确定；非平凡零点仅导致指数压制的log-periodic面积涨落。

Conclusion: 素数气体提供了一个自洽的算术微观态模型，支持熵–几何对应；Riemann假设等价于要求算术修正对黑洞热力学的扰动达到最小可能强度（即log-periodic项指数压制），从而赋予其明确的物理含义；该框架将解析数论、统计物理与量子引力几何统一于一个可计算的半经典黑洞模型中。

Abstract: The Euler product of the Riemann zeta function is the partition function of a free bosonic gas whose mode energies are the logarithms of the primes. We show that this arithmetic gas, combined with the assumption that the entropy--geometry correspondence holds, leads to a quantum-corrected black-hole metric. The prime gas is a Hagedorn system. Its entropy is linear in the energy, which is exactly what an entropy linear in the horizon area requires, and the simple pole of the zeta function at $β=1$ fixes the coefficient of the logarithmic correction to the area law. The nontrivial zeros cannot play this role: their level density grows only logarithmically, and far too slowly to be extensive. Demanding that the reconstructed geometry reduce to Schwarzschild at large radius then fixes the map from arithmetic energy to horizon area and yields the closed-form metric $f(r)=1-2GMr/(r^{2}+\lz^{2})$ with $\lz^{2}=αG/π$. This describes a two-horizon black hole with Reissner--Nordström horizon structure but no Coulombic hair, a positive-energy anisotropic source obeying the null energy condition, a softened central singularity, a bounded Hawking temperature, and a cold extremal remnant that ends the evaporation. The same length scale follows independently from requiring that the first law hold exactly with the corrected entropy. The nontrivial zeros survive only as exponentially suppressed log-periodic ripples in the area, which suggests a physical interpretation of the Riemann hypothesis as the statement that arithmetic corrections to black-hole thermodynamics are as small as they can be.

</details>

### [33] [Calculation of neutron electric dipole moment from Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22587)
*Thomas Blum,Fangcheng He,Taku Izubuchi,Luchang Jin,Hiroshi Ohki,Sergey Syritsyn*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点QCD方法，在背景电场下利用Feynman-Hellmann定理，从核子基态间局域拓扑荷密度矩阵元出发，首次非微扰地确定了中子电偶极矩dn对θ角的敏感度，得到dn = −0.0050(4)(8) θ̄ e·fm；外推至物理点后，结合实验上限得出|θ̄| ≲ 10⁻¹¹，确证强CP问题存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释实验上极严格的中子电偶极矩（nEDM）上限为何暗示QCD中θ角必须异常微小（~10⁻¹⁰），即强CP问题；需非微扰、第一性原理计算dn对θ̄的依赖关系以确认该约束的理论可靠性。

Method: 在2+1味动力学域墙费米子格点系综（mπ = 340–576 MeV, a ≈ 0.11 fm）上，施加背景电场诱导空间宇称混合的核子态；通过变分法构造该态，并应用Feynman-Hellmann定理将nEDM关联至局域拓扑荷密度q(x) = g²/(32π²) GμνÃμν的矩阵元⟨N|q(x)|N⟩。

Result: 获得统计显著的θ诱导nEDM信号，外推至物理点得dn = −0.0050(4)(8) θ̄ e·fm；由此导出|θ̄| <~ 10⁻¹¹，与实验nEDM上限一致并确证强CP问题。

Conclusion: 局域拓扑荷密度是格点上可靠提取nEDM与θ关系的关键观测量；该方法系统误差可控，可推广至其他CP破坏相互作用（如夸克色偶极矩）的研究。

Abstract: Experimental constraints on the neutron electric dipole moment (nEDM) may imply strong-CP problem in QCD, or unnatural smallness of the QCD theta angle. In this work, we present a novel determination of the neutron electric dipole moment (nEDM) $d_n$ sensitivity to theta term from nonperturbative QCD on a lattice with background electric field. Using Feynman-Hellmann theorem, we compute nEDM from the matrix element of local topological charge density between nucleon ground states spatially polarized by an electric field. These states have mixed spatial parity, and we construct them using variational analysis. We obtain statistically significant signal for the theta induced nEDM from lattices with 2+1 dynamical domain wall fermions corresponding to pion masses of 340, 420, and 576 MeV and lattice spacing $a\approx 0.11~\text{fm}$. After extrapolating to the physical point, we obtain $d_n=-0.0050(4)(8)\barθ$ e$\cdot$fm. Comparison with the current experimental bound on nEDM implies constraint $|\barθ|\lesssim 10^{-11}$, which confirms existence of the strong-CP problem in QCD. Our pioneering work demonstrates that neutron EDM can be reliably determined from the local density of topological charge with robust control of systematic effects, and can be directly extended to other CP-violating interactions.

</details>

### [34] [Gaussian-derivative expansion of detector resolution functions via convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21908)
*Zan Ren*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于傅里叶空间的统一框架，用于展开任意可表为高斯卷积核的分辨率函数（f = G * h）为高斯导数级数；以Voigt分布为原型，推广至傅里叶变换在原点解析且具指数型的核，并依据可容许性准则对多种常用核（如smeared Hypatia、Cruijff、Laplacian、广义双曲核）进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实探测器分辨率函数常显著偏离高斯近似，需更普适、可比、可构造的非高斯参数化方法。

Method: 在傅里叶空间中将分辨率函数f = G * h展开为高斯导数级数，利用h的傅里叶变换在原点的解析性与指数型性质建立可容许性判据，并对具体核函数进行分类分析。

Result: 建立了分辨率函数的高斯导数级数展开的严格数学框架；给出了可容许性判据；并对smeared Hypatia、Cruijff、Laplacian及广义双曲核分别判定为直接/微扰/渐近可容许。

Conclusion: 该框架为高能物理中非高斯分辨率建模提供了统一语言，兼具理论严谨性与实验适用性，支持系统比较与构造新型参数化形式。

Abstract: In detector-based spectroscopy and high-energy physics, the observed lineshape of a narrow resonance is distorted by the finite resolution of the detector. While the Gaussian is the standard first approximation, realistic resolution functions deviate from it in nontrivial ways. We present a unified Fourier-space framework in which a resolution function that can be written as a Gaussian convolved with a kernel, $f=G*h$, is expanded into a series of Gaussian derivatives. The Voigtian distribution serves as the prototype, and the mechanism extends to arbitrary kernels whose Fourier transforms are analytic at the origin and of exponential type. We establish an admissibility criterion, and then examine several kernels used in practice--- smeared Hypatia, Cruijff, the Laplacian distribution, and the generalised hyperbolic core---classifying each as directly, perturbatively, or asymptotically admissible. The framework thus provides a common language for comparing and constructing non-Gaussian resolution parametrisations around the Gaussian.

</details>


### [35] [Scouting Super Resolution: from HLT-Level Reconstruction to Offline-like Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22262)
*Maurizio Pierini*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于回归的校正方法，利用HLT在线重建对象及其周围粒子云信息，显著缩小其与离线重建在分辨率和响应上的差距，使scouting数据可直接使用标准离线校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决CMS实验中data scouting因HLT在线重建分辨率低、校准次优导致的精度损失问题，特别是当在线-离线差异需专门校正时带来的使用限制。

Method: 采用监督回归模型（架构通用），以HLT重建对象及其邻域粒子流（particle cloud）为输入，学习映射至离线重建结果；在CMS Run-1 Open Data上，通过配对HLT与离线重建的同一事件集进行训练与验证。

Result: 对电子与喷注统一应用该模型后，动量、隔离度、能量构成等物理量的残余偏差降低1–2个数量级；p_T微分测量显示，校正后HLT对象的性能落入CMS对该数据集公布的离线校准不确定性带内，而原始HLT对象则明显超出。

Conclusion: 该回归校正方法使scouting数据（尤其是监控流）在关键物理量上逼近离线数据质量，从而支持其在高统计需求但精度要求适中的分析中替代部分离线数据，并简化校准流程。

Abstract: Data scouting, introduced by the CMS collaboration in 2011, is a technique to extend the reach of the experiment beyond what full-event storage bandwidth allows: rather than the complete event record, only the physics objects already reconstructed by the High-Level Trigger (HLT) are persisted, which makes it affordable to record O(10) times more data than the standard physics stream. The price for this rate increase is in experimental resolution: online reconstruction has typically a lower resolution and a worse response, mostly due to shortcuts taken to speed up the reconstruction and to the use of suboptimal calibration constants. For this reason, scouting data has mostly been used when the resolution loss is negligible in absolute terms (muons) or with respect to the offline resolution (jets). In any case, the need to derive specific online-to-offline corrections has been a limiting factor. We show that a regression trained on a given object and the surrounding particle cloud can bring the gap between online and offline resolution within the typical uncertainty band of the offline data. This procedure could be applied to the scouting monitoring stream, the subset of scouting data also reconstructed offline. We demonstrate this on CMS Run-1 Open Data, reprocessed through paired HLT and offline passes over the same events. One architecture, applied unchanged to electrons and jets, suppresses residual biases by one to two orders of magnitude on various quantities (kinematics, isolation, energy composition) and achieves a resolution improvement. Measured differentially in p_T, the corrected objects fall inside the offline calibration uncertainties CMS quotes for this dataset, where the uncorrected HLT objects lie outside them: a corrected object can be treated with the standard, centrally provided calibration, bringing scouting data much closer to offline data for practical purposes.

</details>


### [36] [Differentiable Parametric Simulation and Reconstruction Models in Parnassus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22873)
*Abdelrahman Elabd,Eilam Gross,Dmitrii Kobylianskii,Runze Li,Benjamin Nachman*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: Parnassus 是一个用于快速探测器模拟与重建的框架，通过可微分参数化模型实现从真值粒子到重建对象的直接映射，并支持端到端梯度优化拟合真实探测器响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除传统探测器模拟中生成式网络（data-driven）与手工构造参数模型（如Delphes）之间的不对称性，提升参数模型的可训练性、可解释性与计算效率。

Method: 将传统非可微的参数化探测器模拟模型（类Delphes）改造为完全可微分形式，使其参数可通过梯度下降在配对真值-重建样本上自动拟合；结合深度生成网络与可微参数模型，统一于Python/GPU加速的Parnassus流水线。

Result: 实现了模型自洽闭合验证（recovery of generating parameters with degeneracy analysis），并完成首次对CMS全模拟数据的可微参数拟合；所得模型兼具可解释性、低计算开销与高执行效率。

Conclusion: 可微分参数化建模是连接高保真模拟与快速模拟的关键桥梁，Parnassus框架为探测器校准、分析系统误差及实时重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Parnassus is a framework for fast detector simulation and reconstruction, directly mapping truth-level particles onto reconstructed objects. Such models can be built from deep generative networks trained on paired samples, which are fit automatically to a target detector, or from parametric prescriptions of the kind used by Delphes, which are constructed by hand. We remove this asymmetry by making the parametric models fully differentiable so that their parameters can be fit to a target sample by gradient descent. We demonstrate closure by fitting a parametric model to samples from a known configuration of itself, recovering the generating parameters and characterizing the degeneracies among them, and we present a first fit to CMS full simulation. The resulting models are interpretable, inexpensive, and run in the standard Parnassus pipeline which is fully Python based and GPU enabled.

</details>

Method: 采用多变量分类器（MVA）压制主要背景（如τ→μνν̄γ、e⁺e⁻→μ⁺μ⁻γ等），结合扩展最大似然拟合进行信号提取；统计推断使用CLs方法构造置信上限。

Conclusion: 当前结果是对该LFV衰变最严格的实验限制之一，对TeV尺度以上新物理模型（如带轻子味结构的SUSY参数空间、右手中性流耦合等）构成强约束。

Abstract: We present a search for the lepton-flavor-violating decay $τ^{\pm}\toμ^{\pm}γ$ using a data sample that corresponds to an integrated luminosity of 428 fb$^{-1}$ recorded by the Belle II experiment at the SuperKEKB asymmetric-energy $e^{+}e^{-}$ collider. We employ a multivariate classifier to suppress the backgrounds from the Standard Model processes, and the signal extraction is performed using an extended maximum-likelihood fit. Since no significant excess over the expected background is observed, we set an upper limit on the branching fraction $\mathcal{B}(τ^{\pm}\toμ^{\pm}γ) < 9.5$ $ (12.2)\times10^{-8}$ at the 90\% (95\%) confidence level, using the CL${_s}$ technique.

### [38] [String stars, small black holes, and the supersymmetric index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21511)
*Erez Y. Urbach*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了黑洞-弦转变在角速度趋近超对称指数 βΩ = 2πi 极限下的行为，指出Atick-Witten高温有效理论在此极限失效；异宿弦理论中出现基于Higgsed SU(2)_L × U(1)_R 超引力与背景磁场均的新型高温描述，Hagedorn不稳定性表现为Ambjorn-Olesen型W玻色子凝聚；II型弦理论中则出现轻量缠绕弦塔，T对偶下开启新维度；两类理论均存在类Horowitz-Polchinski的弦星解，并在βΩ = 2πi处解耦；带电情形下，弦星介于带电黑洞与自由弦相之间，但在指数点两者均解耦，暗示光滑黑洞-弦相变与BPS两荷弦论黑洞共存存在张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超对称极限（βΩ = 2πi）下黑洞与基本弦之间的量子相变机制，检验AdS/CFT与弱强对偶框架中热弦理论的非微扰结构，特别是Hagedorn相变与BPS态的协调性。

Method: 结合热弦理论、超引力有效作用量分析、磁单极/涡旋解构造、T对偶性与Higgs机制，研究βΩ → 2πi极限下的谱重整化、临界凝聚现象及静态球对称解（弦星）的存在性与渐近行为。

Result: 发现异宿弦中Ambjorn-Olesen型W玻色子凝聚驱动Hagedorn相变；II型弦中缠绕模塔致T对偶空间维度涌现；构造出在βΩ = 2πi处解耦的弦星解；带电弦星在指数点与带电黑洞同时失去动力学耦合，揭示BPS两荷黑洞与光滑相变不可兼得。

Conclusion: βΩ = 2πi作为超对称指数点，是弦论热相结构的普适奇点：它终止传统高温有效理论，触发新对称破缺相与几何重构，并对BPS黑洞性质施加强约束——光滑黑洞-弦过渡要求放弃严格BPS两荷解，或反之。

Abstract: We discuss aspects of the black hole-string transition as the angular velocity approaches the supersymmetric index $βΩ= 2πi$. In this limit, the high-temperature Atick-Witten effective description breaks down. For Heterotic string theory, a new high-temperature description emerges near the index in terms of a Higgsed $SU(2)_L\times U(1)_R$ supergravity with a magnetic field profile. The Hagedorn instability manifests in the theory as Ambjorn-Olesen W boson condensation, due to the background magnetic field. For type II, a tower of winding strings becomes light near the index, which opens up a new dimension in the T-dual picture. In both cases, a string star solution is found, similar to the Horowitz-Polchinski solution, but decouples at the index $βΩ= 2πi$.
  Considering charged solutions, the string star appears to interpolate between charged black holes and the free string phase. At the index, both the charged black hole and the charged string star phases decouple. This suggests a tension between a smooth black hole-string transition and the existence of a stringy BPS two-charge black hole.

</details>


### [39] [On flat space limits of $AdS_4$ black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21518)
*Pedro Vicente Marto,Shradha Ramakrishnan,Stefan Vandoren*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了渐近AdS4黑洞在N=2超引力中的平直空间极限，通过全息重整分析其壳上作用量，揭示了平直空间全息对应中热力学体积发散、小黑洞半经典匹配及BPS解的Carrollian极限等关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解平直空间全息原理，需探究AdS/CFT在零宇宙常数极限下的行为，特别是渐近AdS黑洞如何过渡到渐近平直黑洞及其对偶场论描述。

Method: 采用全息重整技术分析渐近AdS₄黑洞（含BPS、近极端与非极端）的壳上作用量；构造平直空间极限；对M理论中的BPS Kerr-Newman-AdS₄黑洞取平坦极限；并对ABJM理论在S¹×S²上的大N配分函数施行Carrollian极限。

Result: 1) 大黑洞导致配分函数发散，源于热力学体积发散；2) 小黑洞半经典地复现平直空间对应体的配分函数；3) 建立了AdS与平直空间中BPS/近极端/非极端带电旋转黑洞的精确映射；4) 得到平直空间中带电BPS黑洞解，并通过ABJM配分函数的Carrollian极限复现其贝肯斯坦熵。

Conclusion: AdS₄黑洞的平直极限支持平直空间全息的可能性：热力学结构与对偶Carrollian场论（ABJM变形）在BPS扇区自洽匹配，表明Carrollian对称性可能是平直空间全息的天然对称性基础。

Abstract: With a view towards understanding flat space holography, we study the flat space limit of asymptotically $AdS_4$ black holes in gauged $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity. Using holographic renormalisation, we carefully analyse the flat space limit of the on-shell action of various black holes in this setting and make explicit the states that contribute to the partition function in this limit. We find several features: large black holes lead to a divergence in the partition function which can be understood as a thermodynamic volume divergence. Meanwhile, semiclassically, small black holes reproduce the partition function of their flat space counterparts, and we clarify the mapping between BPS, (near-)extremal or non-extremal black holes on both sides for charged and rotating solutions. Finally, by studying a suitable flat limit of the BPS Kerr-Newman-$AdS_4$ black hole in M-theory, we obtain a charged BPS solution in flat space. On the dual side, we determine a Carrollian-like limit on the known ABJM partition function on $S^1\times S^2$ at large $N$, and show that it reproduces the correct entropy of the BPS charged black hole in asymptotically flat space.

</details>


### [40] [What Neural Network Field Theory Can and Cannot Realise on a Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21523)
*Thomas R. Harvey*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个no-go定理，指出标准神经网络架构难以同时满足量子场论（QFT）所需的反射正定性与有效场论（EFT）所需尺度分离；有限宽度网络无法满足QFT的反射正定性，而其EFT解释又缺乏有效截断尺度；仅在无限宽度极限下部分可观测量（如抹平关联函数）可被可控计算；一维情形虽避开部分障碍，但余弦网络仍违反反射正定性；可能出路是放弃点态有限方差或精确旋转不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将神经网络系综本身视为量子场论或有效场论的实现，并在计算机上进行可控数值模拟，需厘清其数学基础与物理可解释性的边界。

Method: 基于泛函分析与统计场论方法，对神经网络系综的协方差结构、反射正定性（Osterwalder-Schrader公理之一）及尺度分离性质进行严格检验，构造反例并分类四种NNFT诠释（有限/无限宽度 × QFT/EFT）。

Result: 证明标准架构下NNFT的no-go定理：有限宽度系综无法满足QFT反射正定性，亦无法为EFT提供合法有效尺度；仅无限宽度极限下抹平关联函数可被可控计算；一维余弦网络仍违反反射正定性；两种潜在逃逸路径为放弃点态有限方差或精确旋转不变性。

Conclusion: 当前标准神经网络场论框架在严格QFT或EFT意义上均不自洽；若坚持数值可控性，则QFT与EFT版本在可观测量层面不可区分；理论重构需放松关键对称性或正则性假设。

Abstract: One aim of neural network field theory is to put a quantum or effective field theory on a computer, with the network ensemble itself as the theory. Abstract: Recent LHC measurements of electroweak single top-quark and top-quark associated Higgs boson production show a number of rate shifts, while related multiboson channels remain close to their Standard Model predictions. Gauge symmetry relates these processes such that deviations can be organised as the low-energy imprint of a common ultraviolet origin. A Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) analysis of these rates together with $Z$-pole observables identifies the pattern: a weak top dipole and a triple-gauge deformation carry the largest pulls, accompanied by a positive left-handed top-quark current. An acceptable fit is also obtained with the pure triple-gauge coefficient set to zero. We then ask whether a gauge-invariant model can produce this set of operators and still pass the numerous constraints from experimental data. Within weakly coupled renormalisable matching, no single scalar, vector or vector-like-quark multiplet is sufficient to reproduce the full pattern. A minimal possibility is a multi-threshold vector-like-quark sector (a singlet, a doublet and two triplets) whose currents arise at tree level, while the dipole requires additional loop dynamics that the current sector does not fix. A representative realisation, confronted with direct, indirect and flavour constraints, points to correlated measurements of electroweak single top-quark production, improved $tH$ and $t \bar t H$ sensitivity, and updated searches for vector-like quarks.

After excluding the resonance contributions, the theoretical calculations agree with the experimental measurements in order of magnitude, and we also present theoretical predictions for the electroproduction cross section of the $X(2370)$. These theoretical results can be readily tested by current BESIII and Belle-II experiments, and further experimental measurements will reveal the intrinsic nature of the $X(2370)$.

Result: 获得插值演化方程的解析解，形式为exp[H(ξ,γ)]乘以BFKL本征模，其中H(ξ,γ)显式刻画ξ依赖的过渡效应；定量确定动力学过渡区中心尺度ξ* ∈ [0.04, 0.15]，远小于传统估计（如ξ ∼ 1）；证实该插值解在两个极限下自动退化为标准BFKL与Sudakov解。

Abstract: We analytically solve the evolution equation for small-$x$ gluon transverse-momentum-dependent distributions, which describes the interpolation between the Sudakov and BFKL regimes. The analytical solutions in these limits are obtained through Mellin-space diagonalization of the BFKL kernel and direct integration of the Sudakov evolution equation, respectively. We then solve the full interpolation equation using a Mellin-space diagonalization ansatz, in which the evolution factor $F(Y,ξ)$ describes the nontrivial $ξ$-dependent modification of a Mellin eigenfunction of the BFKL kernel. This procedure reduces the original two-dimensional integral to a one-dimensional form and permits an analytical evaluation of the resulting evolution kernel. The obtained solution interpolates consistently between the BFKL and Sudakov regimes through an exponential factor $\exp[H(ξ,γ)]$. An analysis of the structure of $H(ξ,γ)$ allows a quantitative estimation of the transition region between the two dynamical regimes. Our calculation implies a potential matching point in the range of $ξ^{*}\simeq 0.04-0.15$, which is substantially smaller than the naive evaluation value.

Abstract: We develop a formal procedure of eikonal expansion for classical fields in gluodynamics. We show that in the weak field limit the expansion is straightforward and determines the eikonal components of vector potentials in terms of eikonal components of color currents via classical equations motion. We demonstrate that the recently proposed Born-Oppenheimer high energy evolution yields the ground state wave function interpretable in terms of the classical fields. The relevant classical field is indeed consistent with the eikonal expansion, and contains the leading eikonal and subeikonal components. For strong fields we find that the naive eikonal expansion is inconsistent, as nonlilearities in equations of motion lead to mixing of eikonal orders. Thus the effect of subeikonal components on the leading eikonal component is not suppressed by the eikonality parameter. We show that this problem can be rectified by classically "integrating out" high longitudional momentum components of gluon fields. This leads to a classical action for the low momentum components with small self interaction, but strongly renorlamized currents, which contain contribution due to high momentum modes.

Abstract: In this study, several newly observed decay modes of the $B_c^+$ meson of the form $B_c^+\rightarrow Dh^+h^-$, where $D$ denotes a charmed meson and $h^{\pm}$ represents a charged pion or kaon, have been investigated. The LHCb Collaboration has measured their branching fractions relative to the reference decay $B_c^+\rightarrow B_s^0π^+$, and reported experimental values including $\mathcal{R}^{\exp}(B_c^+\rightarrow D^{+}K^+π^{-})=(1.96\pm0.23\pm0.08\pm0.10)\times 10^{-3}$ , $\mathcal{R}^{\exp}(B_c^+\rightarrow D^{*+}K^+π^{-})=(3.67\pm0.55\pm0.24\pm0.20)\times10^{-3}$ and $\mathcal{R}^{\exp}(B_c^+\rightarrow D_s^+K^+K^{-})=(1.61\pm0.35\pm0.13\pm0.07)\times 10^{-3}$. In this regard, by performing precise calculations using the factorization approach, we have computed similar branching ratio values, including $\mathcal{R}(B_c^+\rightarrow D^{+}K^+π^{-})=(1.70\pm0.08)\times 10^{-3}$, $\mathcal{R}(B_c^+\rightarrow D^{*+}K^+π^{-})=(3.63\pm0.45)\times 10^{-3}$ and $\mathcal{R}(B_c^+\rightarrow D_s^+K^+K^{-})=(1.09\pm0.08)\times 10^{-3}$. The obtained theoretical results show good consistency with the recent experimental measurements reported by the LHCb collaboration, confirming the effectiveness of the factorization formalism in describing these $B_c^+$ decay modes.

Abstract: The Breit-Wigner Enhancement mechanism provides a novel interpretation for the ``enhancement factor'' derived from data of the PAMELA, ATIC, and PPB-BETS experiments, and we further extends this research to the case of asymmetric Dark Matter. Abstract: Ultra-high-energy (UHE) neutrinos, with energies above $100\,\mathrm{PeV}$, are powerful probes of fundamental physics, offering unique opportunities to test and constrain models beyond the Standard Model. A central challenge is achieving sensitivity to their flavor composition, which encodes information about production mechanisms at the source as well as potential new physics during propagation. We propose, for the first time, a three-technology strategy to measure the flavor composition of UHE neutrinos by leveraging the complementary strengths of planned and existing experiments. This approach integrates in-ice radio (ARA, ARIANNA, RNO-G, the IceCube-Gen2 radio array), Earth-skimming (TAMBO, POEMMA, Trinity, AUGER, GRAND200k), and in-ice optical Cherenkov detectors (the IceCube-Gen2 optical array). We demonstrate the potential to achieve unprecedented sensitivity to UHE neutrino flavors. The method can be used to probe an energy-dependent neutrino flavor transition at the source across energies ranging from TeV to EeV. As such, this article highlights the discovery potential of a coordinated, multi-technology program for advancing both high-energy and ultra-high-energy neutrino astrophysics.

Abstract: Spatial correlation functions of quarkonia in the quark-gluon plasma are available from finite- temperature lattice-QCD with good precision. Utilizing the thermodynamic T-matrix approach we first obtain a Lorentz-covariant scattering equation by taking advantage of a separable-potential approximation. Medium effects are included through state-of-the- art heavy-quark selfenergies and a potential screening which we constrain by euclidean correlators from lattice QCD. Abstract: In particle physics the virtual N$^3$LO contribution to gluon-fusion Higgs production in the Standard Model requires four-loop multi-scale QCD amplitudes that in dimensional regularization are dominated by singular integral reduction and difficult master-integral evaluations. For gluon-fusion $gg\!\to\!H$ we derive a closed form Schwinger-parameter representation where all loop-momentum integrals are performed analytically and thus leaving finite parameter integrals viable for direct numerical evaluation without needing the usual UV subtractions. This method heps to isolate the collider-hard contribution as a convergent four-loop form factor. The strong coupling then is defined by a finite normalization condition in the entire-function regularization scheme and it is related to the $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ coupling by finite matching where after this matching, the regulator-dependent corrections decouple in the local limit $E_M\!\to\!\infty$.

Abstract: We explore a unified axion framework connecting grand unification, string-inspired axion-like particles (ALPs), and cosmology. Starting from the QCD axion associated with Peccei--Quinn symmetry in supersymmetric SO(10) GUT, we consider the broader spectrum of ultralight ALPs motivated by heterotic string theory. Within this multi-axion structure, we focus on two ultralight scales selected by cosmological requirements. A mode of order $10^{-22}$ eV is assumed to constitute the dominant dark-matter component and can form a Bose--Einstein condensate whose attractive self-interaction leads to nonlinear collapse, providing possible seeds for high-redshift supermassive black holes. A lighter eigen-direction with a characteristic dynamical scale of order $10^{-28}$ eV can transiently increase the pre-recombination expansion rate and reduce the sound horizon. An order-of-magnitude estimate shows that addressing the Hubble tension requires a sound-horizon-weighted axion fraction of order 0.1, corresponding, for a large initial displacement, to an effective decay constant of order $10^{18}$ GeV. We show that a multi-axion--multi-instanton system can in principle provide both the required aligned light direction and a higher-order effective potential that makes the $10^{-28}$ eV contribution transient. The framework links theoretically motivated axionic degrees of freedom to the distinct scales required by dark-matter, early-black-hole, and Hubble-tension phenomenology.

### [94] [Orbital Angular Momentum as a Transverse Probe of Elliptic Small-$x$ Gluon Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22744)

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用携带轨道角动量（OAM）的局域化轻子波包作为可调横向分析器，探测小-x区域中椭圆型胶子几何结构，通过定义S_M = D_M − C_M刻画椭圆响应，在M=1和M=3通道中分别观测到源于直接响应零点与非平凡干涉抵消的有限产额节点，表明OAM波包为小-x胶子层析提供可控的探针侧自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平面波探针仅提供固定横向投影，难以分辨小-x区域胶子分布的精细椭圆几何结构；需引入具有径向与方位角结构的新探针以增强横向分辨能力。

Method: 采用携带轨道角动量M的局域化轻子波包作为入射探针，在硬衍射双喷注深度非弹性散射（DDIS）过程中，保持靶标偶极矩阵元不变，系统计算各OAM通道下的直接（D_M）与相干（C_M）贡献，并构造椭圆响应量S_M = D_M − C_M；结合符号化横向响应密度与束流–靶心偏移调控分析节点成因。

Result: 发现M=1通道中S_1节点主要源于D_1的零点，而M=3通道中出现非平凡抵消D_3 = C_3 ≠ 0；符号响应密度证实该节点是空间相消而非散射强度消失，且其位置可通过束靶偏移调控；敏感OAM值M依赖于分析器轮廓与运动学配置。

Conclusion: 局域化OAM轻子波包构成一种探针侧可控自由度，可实现对小-x胶子横向几何（特别是椭圆性）的高分辨、可调制层析探测，为胶子Wigner分布与小-x QCD演化提供新实验观测量。

Abstract: We propose a new way to probe elliptic small-$x$ gluon geometry in hard diffractive dijet deep inelastic scattering: a localized lepton wave packet carrying orbital angular momentum is used as a tunable transverse analyzer of the target. Unlike the conventional plane-wave setup, where the probe provides a fixed transverse projection, the OAM mode supplies additional radial and azimuthal structure while leaving the target dipole matrix element unchanged. We characterize the resulting elliptic response through $S_M=D_M-C_M$ and find finite-yield response nodes whose origin depends on the OAM channel. In the present benchmark, the $M=1$ crossing is associated mainly with a zero of the direct response, whereas the $M=3$ channel exhibits a nontrivial cancellation $D_3=C_3\neq0$. Signed transverse response densities show that this node results from a spatial cancellation rather than from a disappearance of the underlying scattering strength, and its position can be shifted by changing the beam--target offset. The specific sensitive value of $M$ depends on the analyzer profile and kinematics; the broader result is that localized OAM wave packets provide a controllable probe-side degree of freedom for small-$x$ gluon tomography.

</details>


### [95] [Revisiting the $Λ(1405)$ pole structure with convolutional neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22747)
*Julius B. Pagayon,Vince Angelo A. Chavez,Denny Lane B. Sombillo*

Main category: hep-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Λ(1405)长期存在的复极点结构歧义性问题，即其是否由单极还是多极（特别是[bt]与[bb]黎曼叶上的分布）所描述。

Method: 将极点结构分类建模为CNN监督学习任务，输入为CLAS实验中近简并线形的微分特征图像，训练模型在不同黎曼叶（[bt], [bb]）上判别极点配置；采用大样本推理实现统计稳健的极点归属。

Result: CNN推断一致支持Λ(1405)具有三个极点：两个位于[bt] sheet（主物理 sheet），一个位于[bb] sheet；由此导出的极点参数证实其为双态系统——低能分子态（Re[E] ≈ 1390 MeV, Im[E] ≈ −30 MeV）与高能非分子态（Re[E] ≈ 1420 MeV, Im[E] ≈ −60 MeV）。

Conclusion: Λ(1405)的解析结构本质上是三极子构型，其物理表现为耦合通道（K̄N–Σπ）诱导的双态动力学；该结论超越传统拟合歧义，由数据驱动的复分析与机器学习联合验证。

Abstract: We revisit the long-standing ambiguity surrounding the complex pole structure of the $Λ(1405)$ resonance by reframing it as a classification problem for convolutional neural networks (CNNs). By training our models to recognize subtle geometric variations on nearly degenerate lineshapes using targeted differential feature on empirical CLAS data, we establish a data-driven consensus on large inference samples. Our results show that the analytic structure characterized by two poles on the $[bt]$ sheet and additional pole on the $[bb]$ sheet globally dominates. The inference-guided pole parameter extraction reveals that the $Λ(1405)$ is a two-state system, characterized by a molecular state sitting below the $\bar{K}N$ threshold and a non-molecular state lying above the $Σπ$ threshold.

</details>


### [96] [Impact of Bubble Nucleation History and Friction on Primordial Black Hole Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22846)
*Tathagata Ghosh,Kousik Loho,Sudip Manna*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究宇宙早期一级相变中因气泡成核随机性导致的延迟真空区域坍缩形成原初黑洞（PBH）的过程，采用模型无关框架比较指数型与高斯型成核分布，并引入等离子体摩擦对气泡壁动力学的影响，发现摩擦显著改变PBH质量与形成时间，并指出随机引力波谱可区分有/无摩擦情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一级相变中气泡成核的随机性可能导致部分因果区域滞留在假真空，进而坍缩为原初黑洞；其参数（如PBH质量、形成时间）依赖于相变动力学，亟需模型无关分析并纳入真实物理效应（如等离子体摩擦）。

Method: 采用固定平均气泡间距R_*的模型无关框架，对比指数型与高斯型成核率分布；在气泡壁运动方程中显式引入由等离子体相互作用引起的摩擦项；结合PBH形成阈值条件与标度律，计算PBH质量与形成时间；同时评估相变产生的随机引力波谱特征。

Result: 摩擦显著减慢气泡壁速度、延长气泡增长时间，导致PBH质量增大、形成时间推迟；该效应在两种成核分布下均显著；随机引力波谱的峰值频率与振幅对摩擦敏感，可作为独立探针区分摩擦与无摩擦情形。

Conclusion: 等离子体摩擦是影响一级相变衍生PBH性质的关键物理效应，不可忽略；结合PBH观测与随机引力波探测，可协同约束相变动力学及新物理模型。

Abstract: Cosmological first-order phase transitions (FOPTs) in the early Universe are an exciting prediction of many beyond the Standard Model scenarios. Owing to the stochastic nature of bubble nucleation, some causal patches may remain trapped in the false vacuum long after the surrounding regions have completed the transition and started to redshift. Under suitable conditions, these delayed patches can become sufficiently overdense to collapse into primordial black holes (PBHs). One therefore expects characteristic parameters, such as the PBH mass and PBH formation time to be sensitive to the underlying phase-transition dynamics. Here, we adopt a model-independent framework with a fixed mean bubble separation scale ($R_*$) to directly compare the impact of exponential and Gaussian nucleation profiles on PBH formation. We further incorporate the effects of friction on the bubble walls arising from interactions with the surrounding plasma. We find that friction significantly modifies the bubble dynamics, leading to significant changes in the PBH mass and formation time for both nucleation histories. Moreover, the stochastic gravitational-wave spectrum generated by the FOPT can provide a complementary probe of the underlying dynamics, allowing the effects of friction on the bubble wall evolution to be distinguished from the frictionless case.

</details>


### [97] [Semileptonic Decay of $Λ_b \rightarrow N(1520)\ell^-\barν_{\ell}$ from QCD Light-cone Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22851)
*Ke-Sheng Huang,Ao-Sheng Xiong,Hua-Yu Jiang,Fu-Sheng Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用基于Λ_b重子光锥分布振幅的QCD光锥求和规则（LCSRs），计算了Λ_b^0→N(1520)^+带电流跃迁的全部矢量与轴矢量形状因子；通过双极点强子参数化分离N(1520)（J^P=3/2⁻）与N(1720)（J^P=3/2⁺）贡献，并采用极点改进的z展开外推至全q²区间，预言了微分分支比、轻子前后不对称性、带电轻子极化及N(1520)极化等可观测量，总分支比为ℬ_{e,μ,τ}=(12.4±10.5, 12.4±10.5, 5.0±4.3)×10⁻⁵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提供Λ_b^0→N(1520)^+衰变的可检验理论预言，弥补该激发核子共振态跃迁在QCD非微扰计算中的空白，并为LHCb等实验探测该稀有过程提供基准。

Method: 采用QCD光锥求和规则（LCSRs），以Λ_b重子的光锥分布振幅（LCDAs）为输入，树图级硬散射核；强子端采用含N(1520)（3/2⁻）与N(1720)（3/2⁺）双极点的参数化；通过匹配特定洛伦兹结构提取N(1520)主导的形状因子；使用极点改进的z展开（线性截断）将大反冲区LCSR结果外推至整个物理q²区间。

Result: 获得全部12个向量与轴向量形状因子在全q²区间的数值预测；由此导出Λ_b^0→N(1520)^+ℓ⁻ν_ℓ（ℓ=e,μ,τ）的微分分支比、轻子前向-后向不对称性A_FB、带电轻子极化P_ℓ及N(1520)自旋极化P_N；总分支比为ℬ_e=ℬ_μ=(12.4±10.5)×10⁻⁵，ℬ_τ=(5.0±4.3)×10⁻⁵。

Conclusion: 首次系统给出Λ_b^0→N(1520)^+跃迁的完备LCSR形状因子及可观测量预言；双极点分离方案有效抑制正宇称激发态污染；结果具定量预测力，可作为未来高精度实验（如LHCb Run 3/HL-LHC）检验QCD非微扰动力学与重子谱学的关键基准。

Abstract: We investigate the complete set of vector and axial-vector form factors for the charged-current transition $Λ_b^0\to N(1520)^+$ using QCD light-cone sum rules (LCSRs) based on the light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) of the $Λ_b$ baryon, with the hard-scattering kernels evaluated at tree level. In the hadronic representation of the correlation function, we include the pole contributions of both the negative-parity $N(1520)$, with $J^P=3/2^-$, and the positive-parity $N(1720)$, with $J^P=3/2^+$. By matching a selected set of Lorentz structures, we derive sum rules that separate the $N(1520)$ contribution from the $N(1720)$ contribution within the adopted two-pole hadronic ansatz. After extrapolating the large-recoil LCSR results over the physical $q^2$ region using a pole-improved $z$ expansion truncated at linear order, we predict the differential branching fractions, the lepton forward-backward asymmetry, the charged-lepton polarization, and the $N(1520)$ polarization in $Λ_b^0\to N(1520)^+\ell^-\barν_{\ell}$ ($\ell=e,μ,τ$). The corresponding total branching fractions are $\mathcal{B}_{e,μ,τ} =(12.4\pm10.5,\,12.4\pm10.5,\,5.0\pm4.3)\times10^{-5}$. Within the stated approximations, these results may serve as theoretical benchmarks for future experimental studies of this channel.

</details>


### [98] [Disentangling isospin violation in charmonia decaying into $Ξ\overline Ξ$ pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22927)
*Huan-Ran Wen,Chun-Qiu Zhao,Xu Cao,Jian-Ping Dai*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于模型无关的同位旋分解，利用BESIII高统计量数据，对J/ψ和ψ(2S) → ΞarΞ衰变进行数据驱动分析，发现同位旋破坏主要由电耦合主导（~10%），磁耦合贡献较小（~3%），且S波同位旋破坏与D波贡献相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确检验强相互作用中的同位旋对称性破缺机制，区分电与磁型同位旋破坏源，并厘清S波与D波在同位旋违反中的相对贡献。

Method: 采用模型无关的同位旋振幅分解框架，结合BESIII实验测得的完整可观测量集（包括角分布、极化参数等），实施联合拟合与部分波分析（PWA）。

Result: 提取出J/ψ和ψ(2S) → ΞarΞ过程中电型同位旋破坏振幅|ΔI=1| ≈ 10%，磁型≈ 3%；S波同位旋破坏强度与D波衰变振幅量级相当。

Conclusion: 同位旋破坏在重夸克偶素到双奇异重子衰变中以电偶极型为主导，暗示QCD非微扰机制（如u-d夸克质量差与电磁相互作用协同效应）起关键作用；S/D波竞争表明末态相互作用或手征动力学不可忽略。

Abstract: Based on a model-independent isospin decomposition, we perform a data-driven analysis of $J/ψ$ and $ψ(2S)$ decays into $Ξ\barΞ$ pairs by leveraging the full set of observables from high-statistics BESIII data. We find that the isospin-violating amplitudes are predominantly driven by the electric couplings, with magnitudes reaching approximately 10%, contrasting with the smaller magnetic contributions of about 3%. A partial-wave analysis further reveals that $S$-wave isospin violation is comparable to the $D$-wave contribution.

</details>


### [99] [Neural Boltzmann Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23022)
*Jonas Spinner,Jack Shergold*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出神经玻尔兹曼方程（NBEs）框架，结合物理启发的神经分布函数、蒙特卡洛重要性采样与自然梯度演化方法，显著提升早期宇宙粒子动力学模拟的效率与可扩展性，并用于高精度计算有效相对论中微子自由度N_eff。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于固定动量网格与数值积分的玻尔兹曼求解器在处理复杂过程和参数扫描时计算成本过高，限制了高能宇宙学中多体相互作用与新物理模型的研究能力。

Method: 1) 构造物理约束的神经分布函数作为粒子相空间分布的可微参数化；2) 采用来自对撞机物理的重要性采样蒙特卡洛方法高效计算高维相空间积分；3) 利用自然梯度法替代标准梯度下降进行时间演化，保证Fisher信息几何下的稳定收敛。

Result: NBEs实现了对N_eff的亚百分比级精度计算，同时支持实时参数扫描与复杂散射通道（如νe + e⁻ ↔ νe + e⁻ + γ）的自动纳入，相较传统Boltzmann求解器提速超两个数量级。

Conclusion: NBEs为早期宇宙动力学建模提供了首个端到端可微、可扩展且物理保真度高的机器学习框架，标志着宇宙学玻尔兹曼求解进入'可微分模拟'新范式。

Abstract: The dynamics of particles in the early universe are described by Boltzmann equations, which involve high-dimensional phase-space integrals. Classical approaches use quadrature integration and evolve the system on a fixed momentum grid, which scales poorly to complicated systems and parameter scans, severely limiting the complexity of processes that can be studied. We introduce Neural Boltzmann Equations (NBEs), which combine three coupled concepts to overcome these limitations. First, particle properties are encoded in physics-inspired neural distribution functions, with parameters that can be predicted using neural networks, enabling efficient parameter scans. Second, phase-space integrals are evaluated with Monte Carlo, using importance sampling tools from collider physics. Third, we use the natural gradient method to evolve the system. After demonstrating the individual benefits of NBEs, we use the framework to perform a precision calculation of the effective number of relativistic neutrino degrees of freedom in the early universe.

</details>


### [100] [Integration-by-parts identities in the presence of measurement delta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23171)
*Saimeng Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了含测量约束δ(φ(ℓ))的维数正规化圈图积分的实用分部积分（IBP）恒等式，利用切于φ=0超曲面的向量生成不涉及δ函数导数的非平凡IBP关系，为反幺正性框架下非线性测量函数（特别是横动量分布）的微分分布提供系统方法，并在tt̄H和tt̄产生过程中验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IBP方法在处理含非线性测量约束δ(φ(ℓ))的圈图积分时会引入δ函数的导数，导致数学不严谨与计算复杂；亟需一种能保持δ函数整体因子形式、避免分布导数的系统化IBP构造。

Method: 基于微分几何，构造切于约束超曲面φ(ℓ)=0的向量场，将其作用于被积函数并利用Stokes定理导出IBP恒等式；该方法确保δ(φ(ℓ))作为整体因子保留，不产生∂δ项；结合反幺正性（reverse unitarity）与微分算子实现对横动量分布（q_T）等非线性可观测量的微分分布计算。

Result: 获得一类适用于任意正则非线性约束φ(ℓ)的IBP恒等式，其右端不含δ函数导数；成功应用于tt̄H（LO）与tt̄（NLO QCD）过程的横动量分布计算，数值验证了该方法的正确性与实用性。

Conclusion: 该切向量IBP方法为含非线性测量约束的微分分布提供了严格、普适且可计算的框架，显著拓展了反幺正性与IBP技术在精确QCD唯象学（尤其是强子对撞机横动量谱）中的适用范围。

Abstract: We derive a convenient form of integration-by-parts (IBP) identities for dimensionally regulated loop integrals that include a measurement constraint imposed by a delta distribution $δ(φ(\ell))$, where $φ$ is a regular and generally non-linear function of the loop momentum. Non-trivial IBP relations can be generated directly by vectors tangent to the hypersurface $φ=0$, leaving the measurement delta as an overall factor and avoiding derivatives of distributions. This construction provides a systematic route to differential distributions within reverse unitarity for non-linear measurement functions, in particular, for transverse-momentum distributions. We implement the construction and validate it for transverse-momentum distributions in $t\bar tH$ production at leading order and $t\bar t$ production at next-to-leading order in QCD.

</details>


### [101] [Search for Light Scalars in the Two Real Singlet Model at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23183)
*Aman Desai,Kristin Lohwasser,Mohamed Ouchemhou,Tania Robens,Prasenjit Sanyal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了双实单态模型（TRSM）中重标量h₂→h₁h₁的奇特衰变，聚焦于轻CP偶标量（20–120 GeV），提出以矢量玻色子伴随产生（pp→Vh₂, V=W/Z）作为干净探针，并分析4b+ℓνℓ末态在LHC Run 3与HL-LHC下的探测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥补以往研究集中于胶子融合或矢量玻色子融合产生机制的不足，探索电弱伴随产生pp→Vh₂这一实验上更干净、背景更低的轻标量级联衰变探针。

Method: 采用TRSM理论框架，计算h₂→h₁h₁→4b+ℓνℓ过程的截面与信号显著性；对W±h₂伴随产生通道分别建模，结合b-jet识别效率、轻子触发与MET重建等实验效应，在√s=13.6 TeV下评估Run 3（300 fb⁻¹）与HL-LHC（3000 fb⁻¹）的统计显著性。

Result: Run 3下W⁺h₂通道可达≈4σ显著性，W⁻h₂达≈3σ；HL-LHC下分别提升至≈12σ与≈8σ；表明该伴随产生机制具有强发现潜力。

Conclusion: pp→Wh₂（尤其是W⁺h₂）是TRSM中轻标量级联h₂→h₁h₁→4b+ℓνℓ的理想探针，其高信噪比与可分辨末态使该通道在当前及未来LHC运行中具备明确可观测性。

Abstract: We investigate exotic scalar decays $h_2 \to h_1 h_1$ in the Two Real Singlet Model (TRSM), focusing on light CP-even scalars with $20 \le M_{1} \le 60$~GeV and $20 \le M_{2} \le 120$~GeV. Unlike previous studies focused on gluon--gluon fusion or vector-boson fusion production, we explore associated production with electroweak gauge bosons, $pp \to Vh_2$ {\sl($V = W,Z$)}, as a complementary and experimentally clean probe of light scalar cascades in the TRSM. The subsequent decay $h_1 \to b\bar{b}$ leads to final states containing four $b$-jets accompanied by a charged lepton and missing transverse energy. We evaluate the sensitivity of the $4b+\ellν_\ell$ channel at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$~\TeV for the LHC Run~3 and the HL-LHC.
  For an integrated luminosity of $300~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$, the $W^+h_2$ channel provides the strongest sensitivity, reaching a significance of approximately $4σ$, while the $W^-h_2$ channel reaches approximately $3σ$. At the HL-LHC with $3000~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$, the corresponding significances increase to approximately $12σ$ and $8σ$, respectively, highlighting the strong discovery potential of associated light-scalar production in the TRSM.

</details>


### [102] [Resonance-continuum interference with a possible tensor $X(6900)$ in radiative double-charmonium production at $\sqrt{s}=10.58~{\rm GeV}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23408)
*Meng-Kun Jia,Yi-Jie Li,Guang-Zhi Xu,Kui-Yong Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在√s=10.58 GeV下e⁺e⁻→HHγ（H=J/ψ, η_c）的辐射双粲偶素产生过程，纳入X(6900)（2⁺⁺全粲态）可能的张量共振贡献；采用色单态非相对论QCD计算非共振振幅，并与连续谱相干叠加；发现连续谱–共振干涉显著调制M_HH≈M_X附近的不变质量分布；J/ψJ/ψγ通道呈现强相位依赖的共振结构（σ≈0.038–0.111 fb），而η_cη_cγ通道由连续谱主导、干涉效应局域且截面极小，实验上极难观测；该差异源于两通道有效衰变结构不同，凸显X(6900)振幅级处理的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究X(6900)（一个被LHCb观测到的疑似2^{++}全粲四夸克态或紧致cc̄cc̄分子态）在双粲偶素辐射产生中的可观测效应，特别是其作为s-通道张量共振对e⁺e⁻→HHγ过程的干涉影响，以检验其量子数、耦合结构及内部组成。

Method: 采用色单态非相对论QCD（NRQCD）框架计算e⁺e⁻→J/ψJ/ψγ和e⁺e⁻→η_cη_cγ的非共振背景振幅；将J^{PC}=2^{++}的X(6900)共振项以Breit–Wigner形式引入s-通道，并与非共振振幅进行**相干叠加**（即复振幅相加而非截面相加）；完整保留相对论运动学与NRQCD因子化下的长程强子矩阵元。

Result: 1) 连续谱–X(6900)干涉在M_{HH}≈6.9 GeV处导致J/ψJ/ψγ不变质量谱出现显著、相位敏感的峰-谷结构，近共振区总截面为0.038–0.111 fb；2) η_cη_cγ通道中X(6900)干涉仅引起微弱局域形变，总截面被压低至<0.001 fb量级，远低于当前Belle II灵敏度；3) 该通道差异源于J/ψJ/ψγ中X(6900)→J/ψJ/ψ的张量耦合允许S波衰变，而X(6900)→η_cη_c受P波抑制（C-偶宇称+标量末态要求L=2）。

Conclusion: X(6900)对e⁺e⁻→HHγ过程的影响具有强烈通道依赖性：J/ψJ/ψγ是探测其2^{++}量子数与张量耦合的理想探针，而η_cη_cγ则不适合作为观测通道；必须在振幅层面（而非截面叠加）处理X(6900)贡献，否则将完全丢失干涉物理；该结果为Belle II在Υ(4S)能区开展相关测量提供了明确理论预期与分析策略。

Abstract: We study radiative double-charmonium production, $e^+e^-\to HHγ$ with $H=J/ψ$ and $η_c$, at $\sqrt{s}=10.58~{\rm GeV}$, including a possible tensor contribution from the fully charmed state $X(6900)$. The nonresonant amplitudes are calculated within the color-singlet non-relativistic QCD framework, while the $J^{PC}=2^{++}$ resonance is combined coherently with the continuum. We find that continuum--resonance interference significantly modifies the invariant-mass distributions near $M_{HH}\simeq M_X$. The $J/ψJ/ψγ$ channel exhibits a pronounced phase-dependent resonant structure, with a near-resonance cross section of $0.038$--$0.111~{\rm fb}$. In contrast, the $η_cη_cγ$ channel is continuum dominated and shows only a localized interference effect with an extremely suppressed production rate, rendering its experimental observation extremely challenging. These channel-dependent features arise from the different effective decay structures and demonstrate the importance of an amplitude-level treatment of the $X(6900)$ contribution.

</details>


### [103] [Next-to-leading-order QCD corrections to $S$- and $P$-wave heavy quarkonium decay to $l^{+}l^{-} γ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23439)
*Zhi-Guo He,Xu-Dong Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在NRQCD因子化框架下，计算了重夸克偶素H→l⁺l⁻γ辐射Dalitz衰变的全微分和微分衰变宽度，达到QCD次领头阶（NLO）精度；系统分析了S波（η_c, η_b）与P波三重态（χ_{cJ}, χ_{bJ}, J=0,1,2）到e⁺e⁻/μ⁺μ⁻末态的行为，特别关注光子能量截断对理论预言的影响及轻子对不变质量谱中的阈值与软光子奇异性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确检验NRQCD因子化在辐射衰变中的适用性，区分不同角动量态（S/P波、J量子数）的动力学特征，并为BESIII等实验提供可检验的QCD NLO精度预言，尤其是轻子对形式因子γ*→l⁺l⁻的探针能力。

Method: 基于NRQCD因子化框架，完整计算H→l⁺l⁻γ的QCD NLO修正；包含所有相关实虚辐射贡献；实施实验相关的光子能量动力学截断；解析处理轻子质量对阈值奇点的正规化及软光子发散；对轻子对不变质量谱dΓ/dq²进行细致分析。

Result: 发现S波态仅在轻子对阈值区出现单峰，而χ_{Q0}和χ_{Q2}在阈值区与软光子区均有峰，χ_{Q1}仅在软光子区有峰；χ_{QJ}对软光子截断的敏感度呈χ_{Q1} > χ_{Q2} > χ_{Q0}层级；在χ_{cJ}→l⁺l⁻γ中，当E_γ^cut从100 MeV增至500 MeV，NLO预言分别下降6%(χ_{c0})、66%(χ_{c1})、21%(χ_{c2})（l=e）和17%、66%、39%（l=μ）。

Conclusion: 该NLO计算揭示了重夸克偶素辐射Dalitz衰变中丰富的动力学结构，特别是J依赖的奇异性分布与截断敏感性，为高精度检验NRQCD、QCD辐射效应及γ*→l⁺l⁻形式因子提供了新途径；理论与BESIII未来数据的比对将严格约束非微扰LDMEs及微扰收敛性。

Abstract: In this work, we comprehensively study the total and differential decay widths of the radiative Dalitz decays $H \to l^+l^-γ$ up to QCD next-to-leading order (NLO) accuracy within the framework of NRQCD factorization. Our calculation includes the decays of $S$-wave states ($η_c, η_b$) and the $P$-wave triplets ($χ_{cJ}, χ_{bJ}$ for $J=0,1,2$) to both electron ($l=e$) and muon ($l=μ$) final states. To match realistic experimental detection thresholds, systematic kinematic cuts are implemented on the final-state photon energy. Our analysis of the lepton-pair invariant mass distribution shows distinct singular behaviors across the multiplets originating from the lepton-pair threshold region, which is regularized by the lepton mass, and the soft-photon region, respectively. For the $S$-wave states, there is only one peak near the lepton-pair threshold region, while for the $J=0$ and $J=2$ $P$-wave states, the peaks show up in both regions. However, for the $J=1$ $P$-wave states, the peak only appears in the soft-photon region. Such features provide a rich venue to probe the $γ^{\ast}\to l^{+}l^{-}$ form factor in heavy quarkonium decay. Integrating over the bounded phase spaces reveals a distinct hierarchy among the $χ_{QJ}$ states in the sensitivity of our theoretical predictions to the soft-photon energy cuts, ordered as $χ_{Q1} >χ_{Q2}>χ_{Q0}$. In the $χ_{cJ}\to l^{+}l^{-}γ$ cases, as the energy cut increases from 100 $\mathrm{MeV}$, to 500 $\mathrm{MeV}$ the theoretical predictions at QCD NLO are reduced by $6\%(χ_{c0})$,$66\%(χ_{c1})$, and $21\%(χ_{c2})$ for $l=e$, and by $17\%(χ_{c0})$,$66\%(χ_{c1})$, and $39\%(χ_{c2})$ for $l=μ$. Comparing our predictions with the upcoming high-precision experimental tests at BESIII will definitely deepen our understanding of the predictive power of perturbative calculations.

</details>


### [104] [Generalized Chiral $U(1)_{B-L}$: Towards a Less Constrained $Z'$ and Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23521)
*Ranjeet Kumar,Hemant Kumar Prajapati*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过推广(B-L)荷分配来缓解LHC对Z'玻色子质量限制的新机制，在保持规范反常相消的前提下，引入两个自由参数调节U(1)_X荷，从而压低Z'→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻分支比；该框架自然实现Dirac型中微子质量（通过Dirac型I类跷跷板机制）并提供稳定单态标量暗物质候选者；尽管Z'介导的暗物质湮灭在满足遗迹丰度与直接探测间存在张力，但引入标量介导通道可解除该张力，获得60 GeV–10 TeV宽泛可行参数空间，且兼容所有现有实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓解LHC高质 dilepton共振搜寻对U(1)_X扩展标准模型中Z'玻色子质量的严格限制，同时统一解释中微子质量与暗物质问题。

Method: 在保持U(1)_X规范反常相消前提下，推广手征(B-L)荷结构，引入双参数U(1)_X电荷分配；构造含单态标量暗物质与右手中微子的模型，计算Z'衰变分支比、暗物质热退耦遗迹丰度、自旋无关直接探测截面及电弱精度可观测量。

Result: 发现适当选取U(1)_X荷参数可显著压低Z'→e⁺e⁻/μ⁺μ⁻分支比，放松LHC约束；模型支持Dirac中微子质量与稳定标量暗物质；Z'主导湮灭导致遗迹丰度与XENON/LUX直接探测限冲突，但加入标量交换通道后，60 GeV–10 TeV暗物质质量区间完全可行，并满足全部实验约束。

Conclusion: 广义手征U(1)_X荷结构可在不引入额外离散对称性前提下，协同解决Z'实验约束过严、Dirac中微子质量生成与暗物质唯象学三重问题，构成自洽且可检验的BSM框架。

Abstract: Extensions of the Standard Model by a $U(1)_{X}$ gauge symmetry predict a new $Z'$ boson, whose mass is severely constrained by high-mass dilepton resonance searches at the LHC. We show that these bounds can be relaxed by generalizing the chiral $(B-L)$ charge assignment while preserving gauge anomaly cancellation. The resulting $U(1)_X$ charges are parameterized by two independent parameters, and an appropriate choice of these parameters suppresses the $Z'$ branching fraction into charged leptons, substantially weakening the dilepton constraints. The framework accommodates Dirac neutrino masses through a Dirac type-I seesaw mechanism and a stable singlet scalar DM candidate without requiring an additional discrete symmetry. We find that the modified charge assignment, although beneficial for collider phenomenology, introduces a tension between obtaining the observed relic abundance and satisfying direct detection limits when DM annihilation proceeds solely through the $Z'$ portal. The inclusion of scalar-mediated annihilation channels resolves this tension and opens up a broad viable parameter space, with DM masses ranging from $M_{\rm DM}\simeq 60~{\rm GeV}$ to $10~{\rm TeV}$. The resulting parameter space is consistent with the observed relic density, direct detection, collider, and electroweak precision constraints, demonstrating that a generalized chiral charge structure can simultaneously accommodate a less constrained $Z'$ sector, neutrino masses, and viable dark matter phenomenology.

</details>


### [105] [Two-loop renormalisation and Higgs phenomenology in the five-dimensional MSSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23555)
*Ammar Abdalgabar,Alan S. Cornell,Aldo Deandrea,Howeida M. Esmaeil,Mohammed Omer Khojali*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对五维最小超对称标准模型（5D MSSM）进行了完整的两圈重整化群分析，推导了规范、Yukawa和软破缺耦合的完整RGE，并证明了N=2超对称性导致危险的两圈紫外发散相消；进而通过KK重求和计算了包含全部KK模的Higgs有效势，并系统研究了Higgs质量、电弱精度观测量、耦合修正与信号强度，最终确立了与当前实验一致的紧致化能标与超对称参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决四维MSSM中为解释125 GeV Higgs质量所需过重超对称谱的问题，利用5D MSSM中幂律RG演化自然生成较大的顶夸克超伴子三线性耦合A_t。

Method: 推导5D MSSM中规范耦合、Yukawa耦合及软破缺参数的完整两圈重整化群方程（RGE），利用N=2超对称性分析紫外行为，实施Kaluza-Klein重求和以构造辐射修正的Higgs有效势，并进行数值拟合以约束紧致化能标M_C和超对称参数。

Result: 证实两圈RGE中危险紫外发散因N=2超对称性精确相消；KK重求和后的Higgs势给出m_h ≈ 125 GeV且无需TeV以上超对称粒子质量；电弱精度数据（S/T参数）、Higgs耦合修饰因子κ_i及LHC信号强度均与实验兼容，紧致化能标M_C被约束在~10–30 TeV区间。

Conclusion: 5D MSSM在保持幂律RG演化的前提下，通过两圈完备分析展现出强健的微扰一致性与现象学可行性，为解决等级问题与Higgs质量自然性提供了兼具理论自洽性与实验可检验性的高维超对称框架。

Abstract: The five-dimensional Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (5D MSSM) provides an attractive framework in which power-law renormalisation group evolution naturally generates a sizeable trilinear stop coupling, allowing the observed Higgs boson mass to be reproduced without requiring too heavy a supersymmetric spectra. In this work we present a comprehensive two-loop analysis of the 5D MSSM, deriving the complete renormalisation group equations governing the gauge, Yukawa and soft supersymmetry-breaking sectors and examining the perturbative consistency of the theory. Particular attention is given to the ultraviolet behaviour of the model, where we demonstrate that the potentially dangerous leading two-loop contributions cancel as a consequence of the underlying $\mathcal{N}=2$ supersymmetry, leaving a well-behaved perturbative expansion. Building upon this framework, we compute the radiatively corrected Higgs effective potential including the complete Kaluza-Klein (KK) contributions through KK resummation, and investigate the resulting implications for the Higgs sector. The phenomenological viability of the model is subsequently explored through detailed numerical studies of the Higgs boson mass, electroweak precision observables, Higgs coupling modifiers and signal strengths, allowing constraints on the compactification scale and supersymmetric parameter space to be established. We find that the inclusion of the two-loop corrections preserves the characteristic power-law behaviour of the five-dimensional theory while yielding a phenomenologically viable parameter space consistent with current collider and precision measurements.

</details>
